| RD0-7 | The review's 7 | The fences had bypasses: the grader contract checked only direct imports and did not forbid the exchange-context read; the read-only union imported stores exposing writers. | `test_reader_rd0_fences.py::test_a_grader_reaching_a_workbench_read_through_any_dependency_breaks_its_fence`, `::test_the_union_read_reaching_a_mutating_store_breaks_its_fence`, `::test_the_read_modules_hold_no_write` | Read modules apart from the mutating stores (`db.stores.pad_reads`, `db.stores.span_projection_reads`); a leaf-level, every-chain grader fence; the union fenced off the mutating stores and running its SQL (and the history's) on read-only connections | fixed |
| RD0-8 | The review's 8 | The `reader_formative` recipe ignored figure predictions (`fpr_` ids and their `:explained` explanations): those gaps fell back to their LO or produced no row. | `test_reader_rd0_spans.py::test_a_figure_prediction_gap_is_placed_on_its_figures_passages[fpr_1]`, `[fpr_1:explained]` | The figure's passages, as `source_context` | fixed |
| RD0-9 | The review's 9 | `INSERT OR REPLACE` on an append-only table's key replaced the row without firing its DELETE trigger (recursive triggers are off). | `test_reader_rd0_guards_migration.py::test_a_replacing_insert_is_refused_and_the_row_stays`, `::test_a_replacing_insert_cannot_rewrite_a_blocks_chain` | BEFORE INSERT guards on every append-only table (migration 264) | fixed |
| RD0-10 | The review's 10 | Every projection scanned all Reader answer payloads (no index on the exchange id), even for practice questions, inside the Ask's RPC and the gap pipeline's transaction. | `test_reader_rd0_spans.py::test_projecting_a_practice_question_reads_no_reader_answer`, `test_reader_rd0_guards_migration.py::test_one_exchanges_answer_lookup_uses_the_index` | A partial expression index (migration 264), the lookup written to use it (no SQL ORDER BY), and no lookup for a non-Reader exchange. 20,000 answers: a Reader exchange's projection 13.71 ms (landed) to 0.02 ms, a practice question's 15.52 ms to 0.00 ms | fixed |
| RD0-11 | The fix round's whole vitest run (after merging main `ca104b4983`) | `SketchFigure.test.tsx > draws a new sample with another seed` timed out once in the whole run (`--maxWorkers=2`, other suites loading the machine): its 12 s wait for the QuickJS walk to leave `t = 0` expired. Alone, 3 of 3 passed (19 of 19); `docs/reviews/followup-fg1c.md` saw the same under load. A test-timing flake, unrelated to the fix round (which touches only `dto.ts` types on the frontend) | — | Wait on the sketch's own frame count or a stepped clock, not on wall time | open (arc close) |

## From the Reader workbench v2 desk shell (RD1, 2026-11-23)

| Id | Found in | Bug | Fail-first test | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RD1-1 | Spec §3.6 (review F9), confirmed while building the desks' two-player test | `YouTubeWatchPanel`'s message listener checked only `event.origin`, which every embedded player shares, so one player's time messages moved another's clock, transcript and reading place (and any page with YouTube's origin could). | `YouTubeWatchPanel.instance.test.tsx > two players on one desk > each takes only its own frame's time messages`, `> ignores a message from YouTube's origin that no player's frame sent` (both failed before the fix); the harness's "two players each keep their own clock" in real iframes | A message is taken only when `event.source` is the panel's own iframe window; the video tests that fake the widget now send from the player's frame | fixed |
| RD1-2 | RD1's whole vitest run (`--maxWorkers=2`, other suites loading the machine) | `SketchFigure.test.tsx > draws a new sample with another seed` failed once again under load (as RD0-11); alone it passes (19 of 19). | — | As RD0-11 | open (arc close; with RD0-11) |
| RD1-3 | RD1's adversarial review, finding 1 (blocker) | "compare" with nothing typed opened a compare reference with neither a query nor a comparison, which the server refuses at validation; the outbox took that permanent refusal for a transport failure and kept the batch at the head of the queue for ever, so no later desk change was saved. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > fix round: saving > compare with nothing typed opens the library pane…`; `outbox.test.ts > a request refused for good is rejected, never retried…` (both failed before the fix); `store.test.ts > a permanent refusal…` | Empty compare opens the library pane. The outbox tells a permanent refusal (`validation_error`, `stale_sidecar_schema`) from a transport failure: the batch is dropped once, said, and the desk reconciled through the store | fixed |
| RD1-4 | RD1's adversarial review, finding 2 | A window's element was nested under its slot's key and focus mode added a wrapper, so pairing, unpairing and focus mode remounted the Reader (drafts, selection and scroll lost), and focusing one pane unmounted its partner. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > fix round: windows are never remounted > a draft survives pairing, unpairing and focus mode…` (failed before the fix); the harness's "pairing and unpairing remount neither window" (failed against the old shell) | One element per window keyed by the window, placed at its pane by style; the measure wrapper always present; a focused pane's partner stays mounted, hidden; the zoom moves per window with rings per slot | fixed |
| RD1-5 | RD1's adversarial review, finding 3 | Any conflict in a batch deleted every batch queued after it, so unrelated changes on other desks disappeared without a word. | `outbox.test.ts > a conflict keeps the batches queued after it, for the store to rebase` (failed before the fix); `store.test.ts > a conflict…` (two cases) | The desk store reads the server's state, rebases each queued op on it (re-stamping its versions), keeps what still applies and reports what does not; serialised against new commits | fixed |
| RD1-6 | RD1's adversarial review, finding 4 | A hidden or offscreen window kept real DOM input: a field focused on a desk left behind still took typing, and nothing moved the focus with the activation. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > fix round: a window that loses activation loses the DOM's input` (two cases, failed before the fix); the harness's "a field on a desk left behind takes no typing" (typed `zz` into the hidden field against the old shell) | Paused windows' content is `inert`; when the activation moves, focus held in a window that is no longer active moves to the new active window | fixed |
| RD1-7 | RD1's adversarial review, finding 5 | The outbox lived in the mounted shell: leaving and re-entering the desks before a flush made a second outbox for the same client (a `seq_reused` waiting to happen), the flush and view-save timers outlived the shell, and a pending view save was lost. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > fix round: saving > leaving the desks before a change is sent…`, `> a reading place still waiting to be saved survives leaving the desks`; `store.test.ts > coming back to the desks` (each failed before its fix) | One desk store per vault and transport, outside the shell, owning the outbox and the flush timer; the shell's own timers end with it and a pending view save is handed to the store; a second load rereads the server and rebases | fixed |
| RD1-8 | RD1's adversarial review, finding 6 | Hidden and preview windows still started background work and polled (cue discovery, library and figure jobs, the reading path, recap narratives and more); RD1's record listed only three polls. | `pausedProducers.test.tsx` (discovery not started offscreen; a library job not read while hidden, the same job read after) and `ReadingPathCard.test.tsx > a hidden panel does not read the path…` (all failed before the fix) | `useWhenLive` before each request of every job reader and timed re-read; interval loops stop while paused; the full inventory is in the RD1 record's fix round | fixed |
| RD1-9 | RD1's adversarial review, finding 7 | A Reader's tag menu and note picker, portaled over the whole desk, stayed open with their backdrop when their window stopped being active. | `SourceWindow.lifecycle.test.tsx > a tag menu portaled over the desks closes…` (failed before the fix) | Both close when the window is no longer the active one, and render only while it is | fixed |
| RD1-10 | RD1's adversarial review, finding 8 | The router saw only DOM typing and focus mode: an intent box armed over a selection lost `x` to the desk's swap, and in focus mode `=` and esc acted on the desk before an open Reader menu. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > fix round: the hosted content's scopes reach the router` (two cases), `scopeClaims.test.tsx` (two cases) (all failed before the fix) | Window content claims its scopes (`useScopeClaim`): the intent box typing, the tag menu, note picker, ask find box and Wrap up the menu scope; the router reads the active window's claims first | fixed |
| RD1-11 | RD1's adversarial review, finding 9 | The shell forwarded an Alt release after a chord it had taken, so the Reader's bare-Alt tracker saw a tap and cleared the capture. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > fix round: one Alt tracker…` (failed before the fix) | The shell's tracker is the authority: an Alt release reaches a window only for a confirmed tap | fixed |
| RD1-12 | RD1's adversarial review, finding 10 | The rail portaled into a panel window kept the source window's React context, so its listeners, lifecycle and esc (Wrap up's "close · esc") belonged to the source, not the panel the learner was in. | `SourceWindow.lifecycle.test.tsx > the rail in a panel window lives in that panel's window scope…` (failed before the fix) | The rail host carries the panel's lifecycle, hub and claims; the portaled rail is wrapped in that window scope | fixed |
| RD1-13 | RD1's adversarial review, finding 11 | A stale close kept its undo offer after the conflict reload, and the client model reopened a window that was already open (RD0 refuses it with `window_open`), duplicating it locally. | `workspace.test.ts > reopening…`, `DeskShell.test.tsx > fix round: saving > an undo offer for a close the server refused goes away` (both failed before the fix); `store.test.ts > undo after a rejected close`; two new `opParity` batches (TS and Python) | The model refuses reopening an open window; reconciling keeps only closed entries for windows not open on the server; an undo offer holds only while its window is still closed | fixed |
| RD1-14 | RD1's adversarial review, finding 12 | `recap_shown` was recorded for a preview, an offscreen source and a windowed source with no rail panel, none of which showed the card. | `SourceWindow.recap.test.tsx` (three cases, all failed before the fix) | The card counts as shown only when the rail is rendered and its window is not paused (on a desk, the panel's) | fixed |

## From the Reader workbench v2 overview (RD2, 2026-11-23)

| Id | Found in | Bug | Fail-first test | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RD2-1 | RD2's harness, when the desks' slot markup was nested | RD1's rule giving paused windows `content-visibility: auto` was written `.desk-window.paused > .desk-pane-body`, but the pane body was never a direct child of the window, so offscreen and hidden windows kept rendering their content (spec §3.6). | The harness's "a paused window's content is not rendered (content-visibility: auto)": with the old selector it read `visible` for all eight paused bodies and failed | `.desk-window.paused .desk-pane-body`; the RD1 two-player check now reads the clocks from the DOM's text (a body that is not rendered has no rendered text) | fixed |
| RD2-2 | RD2's adversarial review, finding 1 | In the overview the reading focus kept owning window input, so a key such as ctrl+z reached a preview Reader (its undo ran); the review also named the Reader's portaled menus over the overview and the unwired menu scope. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > RD2 fix round: input in the overview` (failed before the fix: the preview heard `z`). The menus and scope: RD1's fix round already closed portaled menus on deactivation (`SourceWindow.lifecycle.test.tsx > a tag menu portaled over the desks closes…`) and wired the menu scope (`DeskShell.test.tsx > an open menu in focus mode takes esc…`) | No window owns input while the overview shows, and the router never hands input to a paused window | fixed |
| RD2-3 | RD2's adversarial review, finding 2 | An exit's two-frame cleanup ran after a newer zoom had started (o pressed during the exit) and switched its `zooming` state off under it. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > RD2 fix round: one transition at a time` (failed before the fix) | Each transition takes a generation number; the cleanup acts only while it is still the latest | fixed |
| RD2-4 | RD2's adversarial review, finding 3 | A resize during a zoom re-laid out the slots while the controller kept writing transforms computed for the old rects. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > RD2 fix round: a resize during a zoom` (failed before the fix) | A new stage size settles a running zoom first; the new layout is laid out at rest | fixed |
| RD2-5 | RD2's adversarial review, finding 4 | After keyboard navigation in the overview the world kept its CSS transform transition, which animated the zoom's camera writes as well (the camera lagged and snapped at the commit). | `DeskShell.test.tsx > RD2 fix round: the world's camera has one animator` (failed before the fix); the harness's "navigated" identity check | The transition is off while zooming (class and CSS); a pan still easing is taken where it shows before a leave is planned | fixed |
| RD2-6 | RD2's adversarial review, finding 5 | A wheel taking over a running spring added its delta to the stale gesture target (0 while entering), so a small step outward chased back toward the reading view. | `controller.test.ts > a wheel taking over a spring…` (failed before the fix) | While the spring runs, the controller's target is where p is | fixed |
| RD2-7 | RD2's adversarial review, finding 6 | A card dropped on, or moved with alt+shift+j/k to, another desk's first slot was appended at its end (the transfer's null anchor means "at the end"). | `DeskShell.test.tsx > RD2 fix round: a card moved to another desk's first slot lands there` (failed before the fix); `workspace.test.ts > moving a window to another desk's slot`; a new `opParity` batch (TS and Python) | `moveToDeskAt`: the transfer, then for slot 0 a move to slot 0 at the destination's next version | fixed |
| RD2-8 | RD2's adversarial review, finding 7 | Nothing recorded "opened beside", so that relation and its gather never arose from normal use. | `workspace.test.ts > opened beside`, `DeskShell.test.tsx > RD2 fix round: opened beside is remembered` (both failed before the fix) | `openBeside` keeps the focused window as `openedBeside` in the new window's view; view saves spread the view, so it stays | fixed |
| RD2-9 | RD2's adversarial review, finding 8 | Two exits could not meet commit identity: to a desk never opened (its cards became content at the commit) and into focus mode (the zoom ended on a slot, the commit went full screen). | The harness's "unvisited" (16.4 % changed) and "focus" (29.7 %) identity checks, and "navigated" (44.8 %), all failed before the fix | Before planning, the destination is laid out as the commit shows it: content mounted (paused), the selection on the target, and for focus mode the full-screen shell and window, which the plan uses as the destination | fixed |
| RD2-10 | RD2's adversarial review, finding 9 | The wheel router passed modifiers already ORed with the event's flags, so a touchpad pinch (ctrl+wheel with no Ctrl key) was never classed as a pinch. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > RD2 fix round: the wheel through the real router > a touchpad pinch…` (failed before the fix) | `classifyWheel` gets the tracked keys only and reads the event's flags itself | fixed |
| RD2-11 | RD2's adversarial review, finding 10 | Reduced motion covered only the toggle: the wheel and ctrl+drag still animated the zoom. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > RD2 fix round: the wheel through the real router > reduced motion…` (two cases, failed before the fix) | `beginEnter` and `beginLeave` take the crossfade themselves under reduced motion | fixed |
| RD2-12 | RD2's adversarial review, finding 11 | An Alt-wheel gesture left the Alt trackers unspoiled, so releasing Alt within 400 ms was taken as a bare-Alt tap and cleared the capture. RD1's fix round made the shell's tracker the only authority, but the wheel still did not spoil it. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > RD2 fix round: the wheel… > an alt-wheel gesture is no bare-Alt tap` (failed before the fix, on top of RD1's fix round) | A wheel with Alt spoils the shell's tracker; its release reaches no window | fixed |
| RD2-13 | RD2's adversarial review, finding 12 | The overview's selection could name no card (the first desk empty, a window removed by a reload), leaving the keys stuck, and a card moved with alt+shift+j/k could leave the view. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > RD2 fix round: the overview's selection is always a card on show` (two cases, failed before the fix) | The selection is reconciled with each workspace change (the focus's card, else the first card); the camera follows a moved card | fixed |
| RD2-14 | RD2's adversarial review, finding 13 | Miniatures went stale on content changes that left the status alone, and a build due 120 ms after the overview opened could insert clones during an exit zoom. The third case (a card moved between opened desks loses its picture) was already cured by RD1's fix round: one element per window, keyed by its id, so a transfer remounts nothing. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > RD2 fix round: miniatures` (the first and third cases failed before the fix; the second, "keeps its picture", passes on main since RD1's fix round) | A mutation inside a window's content marks its picture stale; builds run only while the overview is committed and no zoom runs, rescheduled after the commit | fixed |
| RD2-15 | RD2's adversarial review, finding 14 | A pointercancel or a lost capture left a dragged card's transform and the drag state (later moves still moved it), and a cancelled scrub stayed mid-way. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > RD2 fix round: cancelled pointer gestures` (the card and scrub cases failed before the fix; the blur case fails both with no cancel and with a cancel on every element blur, which the harness's "four ways back: click" caught) | pointercancel, a capture lost mid-gesture and the window losing focus put the card back and settle a scrub | fixed |
| RD2-16 | RD2's adversarial review, finding 15 | A ctrl+drag starting on a sliver could not scrub: slivers were excluded as content though §5.5 counts them as background. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > RD2 fix round: cancelled pointer gestures > ctrl+drag starting on a sliver…` (failed before the fix) | A sliver is background for ctrl+drag; the click ending such a scrub is the scrub's | fixed |

## From the Reader workbench v2 companion and search review (RD3, Codex Astra, 2026-11-23; backend half of one fix round)

Ids follow the review's finding numbers, so the frontend half's rows join this table without a clash.

| Id | Found in | Bug | Fail-first test | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RD3-1 | The review's 1 (blocker) | Polled jobs and their records were kept per context, not per vault: after a vault switch a poll of vault A's id returned A's answer, passages or notes while B was open, a repeated id consulted A's record, and `load()` never ended the background epoch, so A's job kept sending requests and completed into B's context. | `tests/test_reader_rd3_fix_backend.py::test_a_job_is_found_only_in_the_vault_it_was_started_in`, `::test_a_vault_switch_ends_the_jobs_epoch_before_its_request_is_sent`, `::test_a_stale_completion_after_a_vault_switch_touches_nothing_of_the_new_context` (each failed with its fix line removed) | Jobs and records keyed by `polled_jobs.vault_key` (the open vault, resolved), checked on every poll, repeat and cancel; `SidecarContext.load` ends the epoch on a switch; a job's `live()` gates everything its completion would do to the context | fixed |
| RD3-1a | The fix round's landing gate (a regression of RD3-1's first fix) | RD3-1's fix ended the whole background epoch on a vault switch, so a hint job of the old vault lost its chooser call (refused, `SpendCancelled`) and recorded no assist in its own vault, where it had recorded the writer's in-flight call as produced and not shown. | `tests/test_hint_service.py::test_a_job_of_another_vault_is_never_delivered_after_a_switch` (`[] != [0]` with the epoch cancel; passes after) | The switch interrupts only the old vault's polled jobs (`polled_jobs.interrupt_vault`: the companion's asks and searches by meaning); other background jobs keep their own vault guards. RD3-1's switch tests still fail without it | fixed |
| RD3-2 | The review's 2 | An answer finishing after `close()`'s bounded joins ran the full reply, whose gap notification restarted the stopped post-attempt worker and whose prefetch trigger queued work. | `tests/test_reader_rd3_fix_backend.py::test_an_answer_finishing_after_the_close_restarts_no_worker` | `reader_ask_reply(live=...)`: with the job's epoch closed, the gap reading is queued in the question's vault (`queue_tutor_question_gap(wake=False)`) and no prefetch is queued; the exchange is kept | fixed |
| RD3-7 | The review's 7 (backend side) | Every note result carried only `panel: notes`, with no note or place, so it could not open the note it named. | `tests/test_reader_rd3_fix_backend.py::test_a_reader_note_opens_at_its_passage_and_a_capture_note_names_itself` | A Reader note or highlight opens its source at its passage (`target` names the note and the annotation); a capture note opens the notes panel naming the capture (`handlers/notes.search_vault_notes` adds each note's reading and passage). The frontend's use of it is the frontend half's | fixed |
| RD3-8 | The review's 8 | The context row was written only after the whole generation: a question whose answer failed or was cancelled before sending had no desk (history read it as before desks), and a failed write was swallowed. | `tests/test_reader_rd3_fix_backend.py::test_a_failed_answer_keeps_where_its_question_was_asked`, `::test_a_question_cancelled_before_sending_keeps_its_desk`, `::test_a_context_write_that_failed_is_retried_and_reported_until_it_lands` | `tutor_qa.ask_question(on_question_recorded=...)` (through `reader_dialogue.ask`): the companion writes the context as the question is recorded, before the model call; a failed write is retried at the job's end and on each poll, and the poll says `context_recorded`. No migration, no prompt change | fixed |
| RD3-9 | The review's 9 | Idempotency records were evicted with results (1,024 kept), so a resent old id asked again, even with different input. | `tests/test_reader_rd3_fix_backend.py::test_a_request_id_is_remembered_after_its_result_is_dropped`, `::test_a_full_record_of_ids_refuses_a_new_id_rather_than_forget_one` | Every admitted client id is remembered for the sidecar's life apart from the results; full (10,000), the registry refuses new ids (`request_ids_full`) rather than forget one | fixed |
| RD3-10 | The review's 10 | No bound on running jobs, and finished results were pruned only when another job started. | `tests/test_reader_rd3_fix_backend.py::test_running_asks_are_bounded`, `::test_results_are_pruned_as_jobs_finish_not_only_when_the_next_starts` | At most four jobs run per registry (`jobs_busy`, retryable; a search's meaning pass says it is unavailable instead); results are pruned as each job finishes | fixed |
| RD3-13 | The review's 13 (backend side) | A notes search by meaning that could not run (the query not embedded) came back as a successful empty group; one group's failure failed the whole job; the job's poll did not carry the groups with no meaning search. | `tests/test_reader_rd3_fix_backend.py::test_an_unrun_search_by_meaning_is_said_per_group_never_shown_as_nothing_found`, `::test_a_group_whose_search_by_meaning_fails_says_so_and_the_other_group_still_answers` | Each meaning group has `status` ok, unavailable (with the retrieval's own reason) or failed, and coverage when partial; `notes.search`'s meaning mode returns its status and reason; `search.job` carries `unavailable` | fixed |
| RD3-16 | The review's 16 | A nested recorder (a query embedding's, a rewrite's) replaced the job's receipt observer, so those receipts did not name the job. | `tests/test_reader_rd3_fix_backend.py::test_a_receipt_a_nested_recorder_owns_still_names_the_job` | Every root receipt started on a job's thread carries `owning_job` in its `budget_json` (`annotate_calls`), whoever records it; still one row per call | fixed |
| RD3-18 | The review's 18 | A newer search from the desk superseded the running one only when it started a job itself: a pads-only or one-character search left the old job delivering. | `tests/test_reader_rd3_fix_backend.py::test_every_newer_search_from_the_desk_supersedes_the_running_one[...]` (both cases) | `search.everything` supersedes the desk's running search before any return | fixed |
| RD3-19 | The review's 19 | The notes group computed `more` from rows already cut to twelve, so 20 matching notes read `more=false`. | `tests/test_reader_rd3_fix_backend.py::test_more_notes_than_the_group_shows_says_there_are_more` | `more` read from every match, then the cap | fixed |

| Id | Found in | Bug | Fail-first test | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## From the Reader workbench v2 companion and search review (RD3, frontend fix round, 2026-11-23)

| Id | Found in | Bug | Fail-first test | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RD3-20 | RD3 review (#3) | With the Reader's intent box on, a selection arms the box, which claims the typing scope with the focus still on the page: `c`, `shift+c` and `/`, the keys a selection invites on the desks, never reached the desk (the box typed them instead). | `keys/router.test.ts > a typing scope a window claims with the focus still on the page…`; the harness's intent run (a real Reader selection by mouse drag: c and / reach the desk, typing in the box keeps them) | The router tells a claimed typing scope from DOM-focus typing: through a claim, the selection's shortcuts (`SELECTION_SHORTCUTS`) still pass; every other key stays the claim's, and a field with the focus keeps all of them | fixed |
| RD3-21 | RD3 review (#4) | A question was sent at once while the desk writes before it (a focus move, a new desk) waited for the 250 ms idle flush, so the sidecar recorded the question against the previous focused window, or refused it with `desk_not_found` for a desk not yet written. | `store.test.ts > an ask waits for the desk writes it depends on…` (2); `asks.test.ts > a question waits for its desk writes…`, `…could not be saved is not sent…`; `DeskShell.test.tsx > a question waits until the desk writes it depends on are acknowledged…` | `DeskStore.settled()` sends what is queued at once and resolves when the server has acknowledged it ("unsaved" offline); the ask waits on it, shown as "saving your desk first", and an unsaved desk fails the question with a retry instead of sending it | fixed |
| RD3-22 | RD3 review (#5) | Poll owners had no removal lifecycle: a window that closed vanished from the lifecycles without being paused, so its question kept polling; a detached shell or another vault left the store's polls registered (`dispose()` had no caller), and a late first reply registered a poll for nobody. | `asks.test.ts > suspended (the shell detached)…`, `> disposed (the vault changed)…`; `DeskShell.test.tsx > a question whose window closes is read no more…`, `> detached, the shell's threads stop polling…` | A closed window's owner is paused, and every owner the shell knew when it detaches; the store suspends its polls while no shell shows it and resumes them on the next mount (a late first reply is held, not registered); on a vault change the old vault's store is disposed and forgotten | fixed |
| RD3-23 | RD3 review (#11) | Retry rebuilt the request from what the exchange displayed: the other selected passages were lost (only the primary span and quote went) and the branch parent was recomputed from the thread as it now stood. | `asks.test.ts > retry resends the original request in full…` | The exchange keeps its request whole; retry resends exactly it with a new request id | fixed |
| RD3-24 | RD3 review (#14) | In the overview the companion was only faded (opacity, pointer events): Tab still reached its input, typing there took keys from the overview's navigation, and Enter cleared a draft the overview refuses to ask. | `Companion.test.tsx > in the overview the panel is inert and the focus leaves it…` | Away, the panel and its tab are `inert` and `aria-hidden`; focus inside moves to the pill (the overview's way back) and returns where it was afterwards | fixed |
| RD3-25 | RD3 review (#15) | Stopping a question rendered "Nothing was sent." as soon as the cancel answered `kept=false, done=false`, before the job resolved, and an already-sent question that later failed kept the claim. | `asks.test.ts > stop shows stopping until the job resolves…`, `> a question stopped before any worker took it was not sent`; `Companion.test.tsx > a stop not yet resolved says stopping…`, `> a stopped question says whether it was sent…` | "stopping…" until the job is done; sent is tracked from whether a worker took it or the question was recorded, never from `kept`; three honest endings | fixed |
| RD3-26 | RD3 review (#17) | The history stopped at its first page (50 exchanges): the backend's cursor was never passed back. | `Companion.test.tsx > history pages through the cursor…` | "older exchanges ›" reads the next page by the cursor and appends it, joining a day or desk split across pages | fixed |
| RD3-27 | RD3 review (#6) | Search kept stale replies: clearing the query below two characters did not advance the generation, so the older reply repopulated the results; closing the panel before a reply cleaned up before the reply arrived, which then installed an ownerless meaning poll for an unmounted panel. | `search/SearchPanel.test.tsx > one search generation` (2) | One generation advanced on every query change and on cleanup; a reply checks it before showing anything or registering a poll | fixed |
| RD3-28 | RD3 review (#13, the UI side) | A failed meaning search read as "nothing found": the panel ignored a terminal `job.error`, kept only `ok` meaning groups, and showed the per-group unavailable reasons only when no group's job was running. | `search/SearchPanel.test.tsx > the meaning pass says why, per group` (3) | The job's failure is said ("by meaning: couldn't finish · …"); a meaning group that is `unavailable` or `failed` says its reason under its name; the start's and the job's unavailable lists are said under the results whether or not a job runs | fixed |
| RD3-29 | RD3 review (#7, the UI side) | Every note result opened one generic, unbound Notes panel ("Open this book…"), even with the note's book on the desk, and different notes collapsed onto it. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > a note result opens its note by its identity…` | With the backend's note identity: a Reader note or highlight opens its book at its passage, the window's view naming the note (`noteKey`, `annotationId`); an inbox note, which no desk window shows yet, is said where it lives instead of opening a dead panel | fixed |
| RD3-30 | RD3 review (#12) | A history anchor (or a citation without a source id) whose book had no window on any desk refused to open ("find it with /"): the shell looked the source up only among the open windows. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > a history anchor whose book is on no desk opens it beside…` | The reading names its source through the existing render read (`reader_render_view` by extraction id, `sourceOfExtraction` on the desk transport); the passage opens beside | fixed |
| RD3-31 | RD3 review (#20) | A selection starting or ending inside a rendered formula lost its TeX: `cloneContents()` held neither the enclosing `.katex` nor its annotation, so the companion was sent flattened glyph text; displayed formulas were sent with single dollars; and the quote was cut at 400 characters wherever that fell, inside a formula too. | `selection.test.ts > formula selections` (3) | The desks' quote is the Reader's `selectionQuote` (intersected formulas taken whole, display delimiters kept); `clipQuote` cuts before a formula, never inside one | fixed |

## From the Reader workbench v2 scratchpads review (RD4, frontend fix round, 2026-11-23)

| Id | Found in | Bug | Fail-first test | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RD4-20 | RD4 review (#1) | A click inside a pad block's editor (moving the caret) bubbled to the block's click, which reopened the block being edited: the draft was reset to the words it had when opened, and blur then wrote those old words back. | `pads/PadStackWindow.test.tsx > the fix round > a click inside the editor…` | Clicks inside the editor are the editor's; the block being edited is never reopened | fixed |
| RD4-21 | RD4 review (#2) | Reconciling snapshotted the outbox, awaited the pad reads, then replaced the whole outbox (and the optimistic pads) with the rebased snapshot: an edit queued during those reads vanished from the queue and from the screen, uncounted. | `store.test.ts > reconciling keeps what was queued meanwhile…` | After the reads, batches queued meanwhile join the rebase (a pad they name is read too) until none is new; nothing is awaited between the last look and the requeue | fixed |
| RD4-22 | RD4 review (#4) | Submitting a block again after a lost reply minted a new `submissionId` (and asked `request_teach_back` for a target again), defeating the pad's and the attempt's idempotency: one submission could be recorded twice. | `pads/PadStackWindow.test.tsx > the fix round > a submission retried after a lost reply resends the same frozen request…` | The request (submission id, version, target) is frozen at its first try and resent as is; a new one is made only after it is recorded or when the block's words changed | fixed |
| RD4-23 | RD4 review (#9) | A teach-back submission whose entry point answered with a follow-up question (`done: false`) was marked submitted and the sheet closed: the question was never shown and the conversation could not go on; a duplicate reply (no entry) gave no way back to it either. | `…> a teach-back that asks a follow-up shows the question…`, `…> a duplicate reply … resumes the conversation from the session's checkpoint` | The sheet shows the question and goes on through `submit_teach_back_turn` (answer, finish now, later); a duplicate reply reads the pending question from the session's checkpoint | fixed |
| RD4-24 | RD4 review (#10) | "Ask about this" on a pad line made a chip with no passage and the pad's window: the companion grounds only in a source or video window, so it refused ("ask from a passage"), even for a line linked to a passage. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > RD4 review: asking from a pad line` (3); the harness's pads run asks from an unlinked line | A pad line is asked with its block (`padBlockIds`) and its words with the question (learner text, never as the passage's): a linked line about its passage, an unlinked one beside the desk's book where the learner reads; with neither, the companion says why | fixed |
| RD4-25 | RD4 review (#11) | Show me polled the figure job in a private loop that ran on after its pad window paused (another desk, the overview), and on completion wrote through a closure captured while the window was live: a paused window wrote. | `pads/PadStackWindow.test.tsx > the fix round > a figure Show me asked for, finished while the pad is paused…` | The job is read through the one poller under the pad window that asked (a paused window's poll waits); a finished figure goes to the window's waiting figures, written only while the window is live, kept for its return | fixed |
| RD4-26 | RD4 review (#16) | Gutter cards were placed against the tab scroller, which split view does not render: hovering or tapping a source, tutor or proposal mark in a split pane showed no card. Tapping a mark also read the event's `currentTarget` inside a state updater, after React had cleared it. | `pads/PadStackWindow.test.tsx > the fix round > in split view a gutter mark still opens its card` | Cards are placed in the pad window's own box, shared by both layouts; a tap reads the mark before updating state | fixed |
| RD4-27 | RD4 review (#17) | The drain said "kept" or "carried" and removed the row's controls the moment a key was pressed: a rejected carry or a pad that could not be read or saved left the success label and no way to try again. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > the drain says carried or kept only once it is saved…` | The drain's actions return promises that settle on acknowledgement (a keep or leave waits for the store's save); the row shows "saving…", then done, or on failure why, with its controls | fixed |
| RD4-28 | RD4 review (#3, the client side) | Conflict recovery restamped a losing edit onto the winning head and retried it, overwriting the other device's words (and superseding their emission); stale removes, moves and lifts were retried against newer text the same way. | `pads/model.test.ts > re-stamping after a conflict: the server's head is kept` (5); `store.test.ts > an edit whose head moved elsewhere keeps the newer words…`, `> the conflict policy > a stale remove is dropped and said…` | Every edit carries `keepBoth` (the backend's policy): on a stale head the server keeps it and lands the words as their own block, marked "written over an older version"; the client mirrors that, reads the pad again and moves edits typed on top into the kept block; a stale remove, move or lift is dropped and said, never restamped | fixed |
| RD4-29 | RD4 review (#7, the client side) | A new or lifted pad joined its stack through `view.addedPads`, a whole-view write separate from the pad's creation: when another device changed the desk meanwhile the view write conflicted and was dropped (the tab vanished on reload), and a rebased whole-view write could overwrite another device's additions. | `pads/PadStackWindow.test.tsx > lift out makes the block its own pad…` (now asserts `addToStack`); `store.test.ts > stack membership by its own op…`; `DeskShell.test.tsx > pads an earlier build kept in a stack window's view…`; the client model passes the shared op script with a membership batch (`opParity.json`, run by the sidecar too) | Membership by the backend's `add_to_stack` (a union with no expected version); the client model applies `add_to_stack`, `remove_from_stack` and `ensure_running_pad` as the server does; pads an earlier build kept in a view join the stack by `add_to_stack` once when the desks open | fixed |
| RD4-30 | RD4 review (#8, the client side) | Production never created a desk's running pad: the dated running notebook, lifting out from it and the companion's "Add this to your pad" (which needs it) were unavailable outside the fixtures. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > a desk with no running pad in its stacks gets one ensured…`, `> a desk whose stacks hold its running pad sends nothing` | The first time the desks show a desk with windows whose stacks hold no running pad, they send `ensure_running_pad` (idempotent); the new pad is known locally at once so its window shows it | fixed |
| RD4-31 | RD4 review (#12, the client side) | "A piece of another source" inserted the search row's label (title and page plus a flattened, 140-character snippet; for a source row, only the title) as an immutable `source_piece` presented as the source's words; the piece in view copied the page's rendered text, clipped. | `pads/PadStackWindow.test.tsx > a piece of another source is the passage's own words…`, `> a held-out passage is not copied…`; the source-piece-in-view test now expects the canonical words | A piece needs a passage (a whole source says "choose a passage"); its words are read whole through `passage.content`, with the title, page, section and time kept apart in the payload; a held-out passage is not copied, and says so; a write after that read checks the window's live state, not the state when the menu opened | fixed |
| RD4-32 | RD4 review (#13, the client side) | A saved quote's back-link, a hover card's "open it beside" and a proposal's "open beside" looked their source up only among the open windows: with the source's window closed they refused ("find it with /") though the passage was stored. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > a pad's back-link reopens its source` (2) | Back-links go through the claims' path: with no window of the source open, the source comes from the stored source id (the block's payload or its link's `sourceId`) or is read from the reading, and opens beside at the stored passage, a video at its time (`t`) | fixed |
| RD4-33 | RD4 review (#14, the client side) | Carried questions beyond the shelf's three were out of reach: the rows were inert, "more in the library" had no list behind it, there was no way to mark one fruitful or let it go there, and the refresh ignored the list's cursor (past the first page none reached the UI). | `DeskShell.test.tsx > the shelf's link opens every carried question, page by page…`; the shelf test now finds the link | The shelf keeps its three (an attention limit) and links to every carried question, paged by `carried.list`'s cursor ("older ›"), each with fruitful, not now and let it go (`carried.mark` at its version) | fixed |
| RD4-34 | RD4 review (#15, the client side) | A passage's ✎ marks came from the pads already loaded and matched exact extraction ids: a link made on an earlier reading of the book was lost on a newer one, and opening only the source showed no marks for its links. | `DeskShell.test.tsx > a passage's ✎ marks come from the passage union…` | Marks come from `passage.union_range` over each live source window's visible passages (aliases resolved; a `needs_reanchor` link marked as such), read again as the view moves or a pad changes, whether or not any pad is loaded; a paused window reads nothing | fixed |

## From the Reader workbench v2 RD4 fix round, backend (the Codex review of RD4, 2026-11-23)

| Id | Found in | Bug | Fail-first test | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RD4-1 | The review's 5 | A teach-back submission wrote its pad row only after the hand-off: a transcript graded at once (attempt recorded, checkpoint cleared) and a crash before the row made a retry of the same submission open another conversation, and recovery recognised an ongoing conversation by its opening words, so two submissions of the same words could be taken for one. | `tests/test_reader_rd4_submission.py`: the five teach-back tests (`…opens_its_conversation`, `…crash_after_the_hand_off…`, `…graded_at_once_then_a_crash…`, `…crash_before_the_hand_off…`, `…same_words_are_never_taken_for_one_conversation`): 5 failed on the landed code | The row is reserved first with a conversation id minted by the pad handler, the conversation opens under it (`submit_teach_back_turn_on`, `begin_teach_back(conversation_id=)`), and a retry finds it by that id alone: completed (its attempt's evidence carries the id), ongoing (the checkpointed state), or never started (the frozen words are handed over again under the same id) | fixed |
| RD4-2 | The review's 6 | A lift-out left the block's links on the stub and read quotes only from the lifted block's new pad, so the lifted block's later wordings were emitted without their passages, a quote of the pad they came from escaped the quote check (emission and submission), and the passage marked the stub. | `tests/test_reader_rd4_emission.py::test_a_lift_out_takes_the_blocks_links_and_its_quotes_with_its_words`, `::test_a_lift_made_before_links_moved_still_carries_them`: 2 failed on the landed code | The lift moves the block's standing links to the lifted block; one definition of what a block's words carry (`pads.emission.context_blocks`: the block, its lift lineage, the blocks they are notes under, their pads) feeds the snapshot, both quote checks and the proposals; pad.get shows a stub's links on the lifted block for lifts made before (read only) and the union read marks the lifted block | fixed |
| RD4-3 | The review's 3 (server side) | A stale edit had no way to keep both texts in one op: the client restamped it onto the newer head and overwrote the other device's words. | `tests/test_pad_stale_edits_stacks_and_passage_reads.py::test_a_stale_edit_with_keep_both_…`, `::test_keep_both_on_a_current_head_…`: 2 failed on the landed code (`keep_both` refused as an extra field) | `edit_block.keep_both {block_id, version_id?, label}`: on a stale head the server head stays and the words land as a new block right after it (payload.conflict). Stale remove, move and lift were already conflicts writing nothing (now pinned); never retrying them is the frontend's half (RD4-20 upward) | fixed (server) |
| RD4-4 | The review's 7 (server side) | Stack membership lived in the window's view (`view.addedPads`), so a pad created against a stale desk version, or a whole-view write from another device, dropped the tab. | `tests/test_pad_stale_edits_stacks_and_passage_reads.py::test_a_pad_added_to_a_stack_survives_…`: failed on the landed code (unknown op) | `add_to_stack` / `remove_from_stack`: membership in the window's spec, one entry at a time, no expected version checked (a union commutes), unrelated view writes kept | fixed (server; the client moves off `view.addedPads`) |
| RD4-5 | The review's 8 (server side) | No production path created a desk's running pad. | `tests/test_pad_stale_edits_stacks_and_passage_reads.py::test_the_running_pad_is_ensured_once_…`: failed on the landed code (unknown op) | `ensure_running_pad`: idempotent, the pad and a stack window holding it created only when missing, for new and existing desks | fixed (server; the client calls it) |
| RD4-6 | The review's 12 (server side) | No read gave a passage's canonical content with its title, location and time apart, so "a piece of another source" used a search row's label. | `tests/test_pad_stale_edits_stacks_and_passage_reads.py::test_a_passages_canonical_content_…`: failed on the landed code | `passage.content`: each passage's unclipped text, page, cue times and section, the source and its title once, protected material withheld | fixed (server) |
| RD4-7 | The review's 13 (server side) | A pad's links carried no source id, so a closed source could not be reopened from them. | `tests/test_pad_stale_edits_stacks_and_passage_reads.py::test_a_pads_links_name_their_source_and_time_…`: failed on the landed code | pad.get's anchors gain `source_id` and `t` | fixed (server) |

Measured differences between the mockup's deep links and the desks' harness in the same state. No fix in this step;
each is due in its stage's fix round. The fail-first check is the comparison script's run
(`scripts/desk_harness/visual_reference.py --ref <the private reference directory> …`), which reports the
difference until it is fixed. The fix round (`docs/reviews/reader-visual-reference.md` §9) set each status.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
