## Reader workbench naming pass

Found while proving the naming pass (record: `docs/reviews/reader-workbench-copy-audit.md` §3).

| Id | Found in | Bug | Fail-first test | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| NOUNS-1 | `scripts/desk_harness/run.py`, `visual_reference.py`; `fixtures/desks/pads.ts` | The desk harness read the browser's own clock while the fixture's pads are dated 2026-11-22 and 23, so from 2026-11-24 the pads run's "the running pad has dated headings, the newest marked today" failed on every branch, and the visual pass compared other dated headings than the reference's. | The pads run on 2026-11-24: `FAIL the running pad has dated headings, the newest marked today` (`["SUN 22 NOV", "MON 23 NOV"]`). | Every harness page and every visual-pass page starts its clock on the fixture's day (`FIXTURE_NOW`, 2026-11-23 12:00 UTC, through Playwright's clock) and runs on from there; the fixture keeps its dates. The other date-dependent readings in the fixture (the drain's day, a label's time, a saved question's since) read the same pinned clock. | Fixed |
| NOUNS-2 | `handlers/companion_ask.py`, the merge of the goal-to-tutor branch with batch 5 | The goal branch read the vault as `_vault` from `ctx.require_vault()`; batch 5's protection check renamed that local `origin_vault`, so after both merged every `companion.ask` with a goal in view raised `NameError` (ruff F821) instead of answering. | `tests/test_tutor_goal_in_view.py::test_the_goal_in_view_reaches_the_tutor_as_its_sentence_and_depth` on the merged tree before the fix. | The goal lookup reads `origin_vault`. | Fixed |
| NOUNS-3 | `fixtures/desks/workbench.ts` (the ink help ladder's fake check) | The fake workbench check marked a model's valid reading "✓", which the sidecar never sends (`workbench/check.py` gives a model's valid reading "≈"; "✓" is only a decided check's), and which the architecture rule on symbolic marks refuses outside `verdictMark`/`claimLine`. | `tests/test_architecture.py::test_only_verdict_mark_and_claim_line_render_symbolic_marks` on the merged tree. | The fake sends "≈", as the sidecar does. | Fixed |

## From the arc K phase review (2026-11-23)

All 26 inherited regression cases failed before the source changes. The span fixture now measures the actual
outbound prompt and schema. Two watcher orderings and lease recovery also failed before their respective fixes.
Tests use synthetic vaults and fake transports.

| Id | Found in | Bug and source evidence | Regression case | Resolution |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| K-R1 | K2.4c B | A completed append receipt precedes the pass checkpoint. Resume checks only the checkpoint in `_run_synthesis`. | `test_completed_receipt_survives_crash_before_pass_checkpoint` | Fixed. The completed structured receipt and write-once checkpoint commit together. Resume validates the checkpoint and gate rejection can still discard it. |
| K-R2 | K2.4c B | `admit_auto_build_job` reads today's receipts once. Later physical requests check only the gate's own settled and reserved dollars. | `test_request_gate_counts_another_operations_receipt_after_job_admission` | Fixed. Each metered request reserves on the shared SQLite spend ledger and links its physical receipt for reconciliation. Its job authorization is checked separately. |
| K-R3 | K2.4c B | The group reserve omits encoded evidence metadata and escaping. The follow-up can refuse after a paid base call. Neighborhood nominations precede singleton preflight. | `test_span_round_with_no_room_keeps_the_base_answer`, `test_singleton_lower_bound_checked_before_neighborhood_nomination` | Fixed. A provably oversized singleton refuses before paid nomination. The actual encoded evidence must fit and the base answer stands when no span fits. |
| K-R4 | K2.4c B | Split appends call `_consolidate_same_title_concepts`, whose key contains only the normalized title. | `test_split_preserves_distinct_concepts_with_the_same_title` | Fixed. A split append folds matching titles only with matching declared IDs and types. |
| K-R5 | K2.4c B | `_apply_concept_merges` writes a client ID into a durable facet parent field. The facet normalizer accepts that field verbatim. | `test_folded_facets_parent_resolves_through_the_surviving_concept` | Fixed. A folded facet resolves its parent through the surviving client declaration. |
| K-R6 | K2.4c B | Shard namespacing rewrites conflict declarations and leaves candidate and disposition strings local. | `test_split_conflict_and_other_pass_disposition_have_distinct_local_names` | Fixed. Candidates, dispositions and conflict declarations share their pass namespace. |
| K-R7 | K2.4c B | `_one_restructure_per_target` selects a winner before `_facet_reword` validates it. Its diagnostic loses alternative wording and citations. | `test_later_cited_reword_survives_invalid_first_edit` | Fixed. Invalid facet edits pass to the normal rejection diagnostic without claiming the target. Competing edits retain their wording and citations in review. |
| K-R8 | K2-20 | In `_run_claimed` a terminal job becomes visible before the revision. Cancellation after the write also skips publication. | `test_completed_practice_and_revision_are_visible_together` | Fixed. The job saves a pre-write practice snapshot. Finishing or expiring the lease publishes a changed snapshot in the terminal transaction, including cancellation. |
| K-R9 | K10.12 | Rich author verdicts remain in memory until after the figure commit. Recovery of a stored figure returns with an empty attempt list. | `test_finished_verdict_is_durable_before_next_author_call`, `test_final_attempt_commits_with_figure` | Fixed. Each execution and attempt has a durable pending verdict on the job. The final decision and figure share a transaction. Lease recovery flushes interrupted attempts. |
| K-R10 | K2.4c B | The preview allocates one reconciliation output and caps input at the neighborhood allowance. Completion derives its count from proposal reuse. | `test_update_preview_prices_split_passes_and_their_possible_span_calls`, `test_completed_append_job_counts_each_reconciliation_pass` | Fixed. Update previews estimate groups and potential span rounds, including cached inventories. The job counts the reconciliation calls actually made. |
| K-R11 | K2-20 | The phone remembers the revision before refetching. Its secondary context can serve the previous vault inside the fingerprint window. | `test_queue_revision_invalidates_a_recent_secondary_snapshot` | Fixed. A secondary checks the files when the queue revision differs from the revision associated with its snapshot, even inside the fingerprint window. |
| K-R12 | K2-20 | Completion publishes on the job type even for unchanged content. The desktop watcher independently publishes a newly observed practice file. | `test_identical_legacy_source_does_not_publish_another_revision` | Fixed. The watcher and overlapping imports share the published digest of practice paths and bytes. An identical rewrite skips publication. |
| K-R13 | K10.11 | `write_withheld_once` marks a key seen before the insert. A failed write or rollback suppresses the next poll. | `test_withheld_poll_after_outer_rollback_has_a_committed_record` | Fixed. A process-stable decision ID lets later polls retry after failure or rollback. Only a committed observation enters the fast-path cache. |
| K-R14 | K10.11 | The pending rich-only branch returns the job but omits its identity from the decision. | `test_a_pending_press_names_its_request_and_job` | Fixed. The request record carries the pending request and job IDs from the returned rich line. |
| K-R15 | K10.11 | Reuse after acquiring the lock labels a concurrent winner as ordinary reuse. The unique-index recovery returns without recording a match. | `test_reusable_winner_between_press_and_lock_is_built_meanwhile` | Fixed. The second lookup compares the winner with the pre-lock observation. Unique-index recovery records the concurrent match. |
| K-R16 | K10.11 | RPC parameter validation precedes the handler's refusal recorder. | `test_schema_refusal_records_only_its_code` | Fixed. RPC schema refusal of a build or revision writes the validation code without accessing the rejected parameters. |
| K-R17 | K10.12 | The first progress report precedes the rich wrapper. Cancellation there or during the wrapper can leave no stop record. | `test_rich_job_cancelled_at_its_initial_report_records_stop` | Fixed. The first progress report runs inside the cancellation recorder, which includes the number of attempts started. |
| K-R18 | K10.12 | Repair classifies spend refusal as model failure. Fallback rendering is recorded before the gate, and the attempt model is captured before the response. | `test_rich_repair_refused_by_budget_records_the_refusal` | Fixed. Repair preserves spend refusal, fallback rendering is recorded when the renderer returns, and each answer supplies its resolved model. |
| K-R19 | K2.4c B | A third summary compares with accumulated text. Pass identity includes the shared neighborhood. A sweep can buy two outputs per group, and provider cache hits are conditional. | `test_split_summary_remembers_distinct_original_multiline_summaries` | Fixed. Distinct original summaries are joined once. The cost note covers both rounds and distinguishes checkpoint reuse with matching requests and settings from provider discounts on cache hits. |

| NBH-1 | Notebook authoring H3, H1 dependency-admission test | A normal marimo pin bump produced the new installation instruction but the regression required the old version literal | `test_dependency_error_is_actionable`; `test_next_pin_changes_the_parser_admission_message`; `test_pin_bump_includes_environment_lock_notices_and_parser` | Compare the displayed exact requirement with the independent project extra. Check installed metadata, lock and notices together | fixed, regression passed in working tree |
| NBH-2 | Notebook activities §15, Track H | The stage list in this checkout gives an H3 acceptance line only. Owner or orchestrator to reconcile the missing list | open |
| NBH-3 | H1 static parser, incompatible cell decorator | Pinned marimo asserts on a class decorated as a function cell. The worker exited and H1 reported a dependency-installation error for a bad artifact | `test_malformed_cell_decorator_is_an_artifact_finding` | Classify the parser's assertion at the parse boundary as an invalid notebook | fixed, regression passed in working tree |
| NBH-4 | H1 loader coverage | Pinned marimo stops parsing at the run guard, so trailing executable source could escape a structure report and the later static checks | `test_unrepresented_source_fails_as_a_format_finding` (trailing import) | Report statements following the parsed guard as a format finding | fixed, regression passed in working tree |
| NBH-5 | H1 report serialization | The parser accepts Python literal decorator options, including a byte-string name. That name could break JSON reporting and produce a misleading dependency error | `test_unrepresented_source_fails_as_a_format_finding` (byte-string name) | Validate the IR options and cell name before serializing and identify the offending cell | fixed, regression passed in working tree |
| NBH-6 | H3 generated cards | Five exported widgets had no signature because their classes inherit the constructor | `test_every_public_export_has_a_card_or_reason`; read back the exported card files | Read the installed class signature when the wheel source has no local constructor | fixed, 112 export signatures verified |
| NBH-7 | H3 toolbox extension | A declarative tool got a card but searching for examples of its declared import path could return none. Example matching and docstring wrappers assumed one of two packages | `test_one_entry_adds_a_tool_with_its_extraction_gaps` | Use the card's public import path for example matching and wrappers, and its source version for docstring examples | fixed, regression passed in working tree |
| NBH-8 | H4 locked-state check | A read of the answer in the same cell that declared the commit escaped the inter-cell reference filter | `test_broken_notebook` with a same-cell answer read | Inspect reads inside the declaring cell as well as references across cells | fixed, failed before correction and passed after |
| NBH-9 | H4 reveal inventory | Removing the release call allowed a visible declared reveal to escape review. A stale entry in the reveal metadata could also refer to no cell or output | `test_broken_notebook` with an unheld declared unit and with a missing unit | Check declared names against the graph and include the declared units in the locked-state audit | fixed, both cases failed before correction and passed after |
| NBH-10 | H1 test scratch | The external-report fixture forced the system temporary directory even when the caller confined temporary files to the checkout | `test_notebook_check.py` with a checkout-local TMPDIR | Respect the configured temp root. Skip only external-report cases and run the other CLI and parser checks locally | fixed, 11 passed locally and 16 external cases handed to landing |

## Reader retirement, phase 2 (the scoped source's extraction)

Found while extracting the scoped source and deleting the standalone screen (record: `docs/reviews/reader-rd-retire.md`,
"Phase 2, step 1" and "Phase 2, step 2").

| Id | Found in | Bug | Fail-first test | Fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RDX-1 | `screens/desks/source/ScopedSource.tsx` (moved from `screens/ReaderScreen.tsx`) | A source rail hosted in a Notes panel window rendered as the Guide ("Reading guide") first: the rail's tab started on the Guide, and opening the source reset it to the Guide, before an effect applied the panel's tab. Under load a reader of the window (and the hosted-rail test) could see the Guide's name in a Notes window. | `windows/SourceWindow.lifecycle.test.tsx`: "a rail hosted on the Notes tab never shows the Guide's name, not even for a render while the book opens" (saw `["Notes", "Reading guide"]`). | The rail's tab starts on the hosting window's tab, and the reset when a source opens goes to that tab (the Guide only without a host, as before). | Fixed |
| RDX-2 | `app/App.tsx` (`openStudiedNow`) | Feedback's "Back to where you studied this" (and the printed page's "open in the Reader") set the book for the Reader without a new open request. The old screen remounted on the book; the desks take each request once by its number, so after any earlier request (a startup restore, a return) the desks opened nothing and stayed where they were. | `app/App.desks.test.tsx`: "feedback's back to where you studied this opens the book at that passage, after the desks already took an open request (RDX-2)" (saw the problem pad focused, not the book). | It is a new open request (the sequence number moves). | Fixed |
| RDX-3 | `app/App.tsx` (the book being read) | With the desks as the Reader, Continue reading after a restart on another place opened the Library: the app knew the book being read only from the desks' report, and the desks report only once shown, so the book restored at startup did not count. | `app/App.fixRound4.test.tsx`: "the remembered book comes back whatever the landing place (item 3) > restarting on Progress, Continue reading opens the last book". | Until the desks first report, the book restored at startup stands for the book being read (a null report, closing the last book, still means none). | Fixed |
| RDX-4 | `screens/reader/useReadingProgress.ts`, `readingReports.ts`, `activity.ts`, `useCueImpressions.ts`, `app/vaultTransition.ts` | Reading evidence could be written to the wrong vault: the app selects the next vault before the desks unmount, and the book windows' cleanups (and their timed retries) sent the reading reports, activity and cue impressions gathered in A to B (review blocker 2). | `ScopedSource.followsLearner.test.tsx`: "RDX-4: what was read just before a vault change is saved in that vault, and nothing of it reaches the next" (saw no report before the selection) and "RDX-4: a report that failed in vault A is kept for A …" (saw it sent while B was selected). | Every deferred write names the vault it was gathered in (`vaultKey()`, the loaded vault's root, null while a selection is on its way): the reading reports, the activity queue and the cue impressions are flushed to their vault before the selection and never sent while another is selected; a failed reading report is kept for its vault and sent when it is selected again. | Fixed |
| RDX-5 | `screens/reader/readingReports.ts`, `screens/desks/DeskShell.tsx` | A book closed during a sidecar outage owned the only copy of its unacknowledged reading report; once its retries ran out, leaving the Reader no longer sent it (the retired F7 case's second step) (review major 5). | `ScopedSource.followsLearner.test.tsx`: "RDX-5: a closed book's report that ran out of retries is sent when the learner leaves the Reader" (saw nothing sent). | Unacknowledged reports live in a vault-owned queue that outlives the book's window; the desks' unmount (leaving the Reader), a vault change and the vault's next load send them once more. | Fixed |

| RDX-7 | `screens/desks/DeskShell.tsx`, `app/dailyPlan.ts`, `app/intentCheck.ts` | A return to an earlier reading's passage (a Today citation, feedback's "Back to where you studied this", the plan's reading) lost its passage after a re-read: the desks dropped a span on any reading mismatch, and the feedback and plan targets dropped their reading (review major 7). | `SourceWindow.reread.test.tsx`: "an open request for a passage of an earlier reading opens the book's new window at it, translated" and "… jumps the book's open window to it, translated"; `app/intentCheck.test.ts`, `app/dailyPlan.test.tsx` (the reading carried). | Every book open translates a passage named on another reading onto the current one (new window and open window alike), falling back to the learner's place with the toast; the targets carry their reading. | Fixed |
